\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
General purpose class hiding global functions, constants and types from the global namespace. Some of them might be moved to \textquotesingle{}ofstd\textquotesingle{} later on. All functions and constants are static and can, therefore, be accessed without creating an instance of this class.

add given element to the dataset. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} element is only added if \textquotesingle{}result\textquotesingle{} is EC\+\_\+\+Normal and the \textquotesingle{}delem\textquotesingle{} pointer is not NULL. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em result} & reference to status variable (checked before adding and updated afterwards!) \\
\hline
{\em dataset} & reference to DICOM dataset to which the element should be added \\
\hline
{\em delem} & pointer to DICOM element which should be added. deleted if not inserted. \\
\hline
{\em vm} & value multiplicity (according to the data dictionary) to be checked for. (See \doxylink{class_dcm_element_a41cb388e0eb83c7d26521119ffd14f9a}{Dcm\+Element::\+check\+VM()} for a list of valid values.) Interpreted as cardinality (number of items) for sequence attributes. \\
\hline
{\em type} & value type (valid value:\+ "{}1"{}, "{}2"{} or something else which is not checked) \\
\hline
{\em module\+Name} & optional module name to be printed (default:\+ "{}\+SR document"{} if NULL) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
current value of \textquotesingle{}result\textquotesingle{}, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if successful, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

Example:\+ 1 or 1.\+2.\+3. Both the integer number 0 and a leading 0 for the numbers are forbidden.

const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \& DSRTypes::\+convert\+To\+HTMLString (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{source\+String}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{markup\+String}{, }\item[{const size\+\_\+t}]{flags}{ = {\ttfamily 0}, }\item[{const OFBool}]{newline\+Allowed}{ = {\ttfamily OFFalse}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

convert character string to HTML mnenonic string. Characters with special meaning for HTML (e.\+g. \textquotesingle{}\texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}\textquotesingle{} and \textquotesingle{}\&\textquotesingle{}) are replace by the corresponding mnenonics (e.\+g. "{}<"{} and "{}\&"{}). If flag \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a7f3e4058b2166bf6355fcefda580b7c0}{DSRTypes::\+\+HF\+\_\+convert\+Non\+ASCIICharacters} is set, all characters \texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} \#127 are also converted (useful if only HTML 3.\+2 is supported which does not allow to specify the character set). 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em source\+String} & source string to be converted \\
\hline
{\em markup\+String} & reference to variable where the result should be stored \\
\hline
{\em flags} & optional flags, only checking \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a7f3e4058b2166bf6355fcefda580b7c0}{DSRTypes::\+\+HF\+\_\+convert\+Non\+ASCIICharacters}, \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_a868fc4ed504a69ad43ddc5b9ba7e03e9}{DSRTypes::\+\+HF\+\_\+\+HTML32\+Compatibility} and \doxylink{class_d_s_r_types_ad5afb30f08e2b4eb1b55f6a2fd1ccbc4}{DSRTypes::\+\+HF\+\_\+\+XHTML11\+Compatibility} \\
\hline
{\em newline\+Allowed} & optional flag indicating whether newlines are allowed or not. If they are allowed the text "{}$<$br$>$"{} is used, "{}{$\P$}"{} otherwise. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} following combinations are accepted:\+ LF, CR, LF CR, CF LF. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}

convert DICOM person name to XML format. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} tags \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}prefix\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}, \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}first\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}, \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}middle\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>}, \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}last\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} and \texorpdfstring{$<$}{<}suffix\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} are used for the XML format of a person name. \doxylink{requirements_The}{The} string is automatically converted to the markup notation (see convert\+To\+Markup\+String()). Two tags are separated by a newline. Please note that only the first component group (characters before the first \textquotesingle{}=\textquotesingle{}) of the DICOM person name is used -\/ see \doxylink{class_dcm_person_name_a341867fa50933b93b95230f4919ee206}{Dcm\+Person\+Name::\+get\+Name\+Components()} for details. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em dicom\+Person\+Name} & person name in DICOM PN format (ln\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}fn\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}mn\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}p\texorpdfstring{$^\wedge$}{\string^}s) \\
\hline
{\em xml\+Person\+Name} & reference to variable where the resulting string is stored \\
\hline
{\em write\+Empty\+Value} & optional flag indicating whether an empty value should be written \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
reference to resulting string (might be empty) 
\end{DoxyReturn}

\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_condition}{OFCondition}} DSRTypes::\+get\+And\+Check\+Element\+From\+Dataset (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_dcm_item}{Dcm\+Item}} \&}]{dataset}{, }\item[{\doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_dcm_element}{Dcm\+Element}} \&}]{delem}{, }\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{vm}{, }\item[{const \doxymbox{\hyperlink{class_o_f_string}{OFString}} \&}]{type}{, }\item[{const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{module\+Name}{ = {\ttfamily NULL}, }\item[{const OFBool}]{accept\+Violation}{ = {\ttfamily OFFalse}}\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [static]}}

get element from dataset and check it for correct value multiplicity and type. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em dataset} & DICOM dataset from which the element should be retrieved. (Would be \textquotesingle{}const\textquotesingle{} if the methods from \textquotesingle{}dcmdata\textquotesingle{} would also be \textquotesingle{}const\textquotesingle{}.) \\
\hline
{\em delem} & DICOM element used to store the value (always creates a copy!) \\
\hline
{\em vm} & value multiplicity (according to the data dictionary) to be checked for. (See \doxylink{class_dcm_element_a41cb388e0eb83c7d26521119ffd14f9a}{Dcm\+Element::\+check\+VM()} for a list of valid values.) Interpreted as cardinality (number of items) for sequence attributes. \\
\hline
{\em type} & value type (valid value:\+ "{}1"{}, "{}1\+C"{}, "{}2"{}, something else which is not checked) \\
\hline
{\em module\+Name} & optional module name to be printed (default:\+ "{}\+SR document"{} if NULL) \\
\hline
{\em accept\+Violation} & accept certain violations regarding the VR and VM if OFTrue. A warning message will always be reported if the value in incorrect. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if element could be retrieved and value is correct, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}

get string value from dataset and check it for correct value multiplicity and type. 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em dataset} & DICOM dataset from which the element should be retrieved. (Would be \textquotesingle{}const\textquotesingle{} if the methods from \textquotesingle{}dcmdata\textquotesingle{} would also be \textquotesingle{}const\textquotesingle{}.) \\
\hline
{\em tag\+Key} & DICOM tag specifying the attribute from which the string should be retrieved \\
\hline
{\em string\+Value} & reference to variable in which the result should be stored. (This parameter is automatically cleared if the tag could not be found. It is not cleared if the retrieved string is invalid, e.\+g. violates VR or VM definition.) \\
\hline
{\em vm} & value multiplicity (according to the data dictionary) to be checked for. (See \doxylink{class_dcm_element_a41cb388e0eb83c7d26521119ffd14f9a}{Dcm\+Element::\+check\+VM()} for a list of valid values.) Interpreted as cardinality (number of items) for sequence attributes. \\
\hline
{\em type} & value type (valid value:\+ "{}1"{}, "{}1\+C"{}, "{}2"{}, something else which is not checked) \\
\hline
{\em module\+Name} & optional module name to be printed (default:\+ "{}\+SR document"{} if NULL) \\
\hline
{\em accept\+Violation} & accept certain violations regarding the VR and VM if OFTrue. A warning message will always be reported if the value in incorrect. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyReturn}{Returns}
status, EC\+\_\+\+Normal if element could be retrieved and value is correct, an error code otherwise 
\end{DoxyReturn}
